+/* ─── Freshness badge (shared) ──────────────────────────────── */
+export function FreshnessBadge({ harvestDate }: { harvestDate: string }) {
+  const { tx } = useLang()
+  const days = Math.floor(
+    (Date.now() - new Date(harvestDate).getTime()) / 86400000,
+  )
+  if (days < 0) return null
+  if (days === 0) return (
+    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-green-700 bg-green-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
+      {tx.harvestToday}
+    </span>
+  )
+  if (days === 1) return (
+    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
+      {tx.harvestYesterday}
+    </span>
+  )
+  return (
+    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-red-600 bg-red-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
+      {tx.harvestDaysAgo.replace('{n}', String(days))}
+    </span>
+  )
+}
+
+/* ─── Earnings summary card ──────────────────────────────────── */
+function EarningsCard({
+  revenue,
+  orderCount,
+  weekly,
+}: {
+  revenue: number
+  orderCount: number
+  weekly: number[]
+}) {
+  const { tx } = useLang()
+  const maxWeek = Math.max(...weekly, 1)
+
+  return (
+    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4">
+      <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">
+        {tx.earningsTitle}
+      </h2>
+
+      {revenue === 0 ? (
+        <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-3">{tx.earningsNoData}</p>
+      ) : (
+        <>
+          <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2 mt-2">
+            <span className="text-3xl font-black text-green-800">
+              {tx.earningsRevenue.replace('{amount}', String(revenue))}
+            </span>
+          </div>
+          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
+            {tx.earningsOrders.replace('{n}', String(orderCount))}
+          </p>
+
+          {/* 4-week bar chart */}
+          <div className="flex items-end gap-2 mt-4 h-14">
+            {weekly.map((amt, i) => {
+              const pct = Math.round((amt / maxWeek) * 100)
+              return (
+                <div key={i} className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
+                  <div className="w-full bg-gray-100 rounded-t-md relative" style={{ height: '40px' }}>
+                    <div
+                      className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-green-600 rounded-t-md transition-all"
+                      style={{ height: `${pct}%` }}
+                    />
+                  </div>
+                  <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 font-medium">
+                    {tx.weekLabel.replace('{n}', String(i + 1))}
+                  </span>
+                  {amt > 0 && (
+                    <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-green-700">₹{amt}</span>
+                  )}
+                </div>
+              )
+            })}
+          </div>
+        </>
+      )}
+    </div>
+  )
+}
+
+/* ─── Farm photos section ───────────────────────────────────── */
+type MediaRow = { id: string; url: string; caption?: string }
+
+function FarmPhotosSection({ farmerId }: { farmerId: string }) {
+  const [photos, setPhotos] = useState<MediaRow[]>([])
+  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)
+  const [deletingId, setDeletingId] = useState<string | null>(null)
+  const [error, setError] = useState('')
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    supabase
+      .from('media')
+      .select('id, url, caption')
+      .eq('farmer_id', farmerId)
+      .eq('type', 'photo')
+      .order('sort_order', { ascending: true })
+      .then(({ data }) => setPhotos((data ?? []) as MediaRow[]))
+  }, [farmerId])
+
+  const handlePick = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
+    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
+    e.target.value = ''
+    if (!file) return
+    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) { setError('Please pick an image file.'); return }
+    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { setError('Image must be under 8 MB.'); return }
+    setError('')
+    setUploading(true)
+
+    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'
+    const path = `${farmerId}/gallery/${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}.${ext}`
+    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage
+      .from('farm-images')
+      .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: false })
+
+    if (upErr) { setError(`Upload failed: ${upErr.message}`); setUploading(false); return }
+
+    const { data: urlData } = supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path)
+    const { data: inserted } = await supabase
+      .from('media')
+      .insert({ farmer_id: farmerId, type: 'photo', url: urlData.publicUrl, sort_order: photos.length })
+      .select('id, url, caption')
+      .single()
+
+    if (inserted) setPhotos((prev) => [...prev, inserted as MediaRow])
+    setUploading(false)
+  }
+
+  const handleDelete = async (photo: MediaRow) => {
+    if (!confirm('Remove this photo?')) return
+    setDeletingId(photo.id)
+    await supabase.from('media').delete().eq('id', photo.id)
+    setPhotos((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== photo.id))
+    setDeletingId(null)
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4">
+      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
+        <div>
+          <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">Farm Photos</h2>
+          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">Visible on your public profile</p>
+        </div>
+        <label className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-green-700 text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer active:bg-green-800 ${uploading ? 'opacity-50 pointer-events-none' : ''}`}>
+          {uploading ? (
+            <span className="w-3.5 h-3.5 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
+          ) : (
+            <span>+</span>
+          )}
+          {uploading ? 'Uploading…' : 'Add Photo'}
+          <input type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={handlePick} className="hidden" />
+        </label>
+      </div>
+
+      {error && (
+        <p className="text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 mb-3">{error}</p>
+      )}
+
+      {photos.length === 0 && !uploading ? (
+        <div className="text-center py-8 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200 rounded-xl">
+          <p className="text-3xl mb-2">📷</p>
+          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-500">No farm photos yet</p>
+          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">Add photos of your farm, fields, and produce</p>
+        </div>
+      ) : (
+        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5">
+          {photos.map((photo) => (
+            <div key={photo.id} className="relative aspect-square rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-100 bg-gray-50">
+              {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
+              <img src={photo.url} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" loading="lazy" />
+              <button
+                onClick={() => handleDelete(photo)}
+                disabled={deletingId === photo.id}
+                className="absolute top-1 right-1 w-6 h-6 bg-black/60 text-white rounded-full text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center"
+              >
+                {deletingId === photo.id ? '…' : '×'}
+              </button>
+            </div>
+          ))}
+          {uploading && (
+            <div className="aspect-square rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 bg-green-50 flex items-center justify-center">
+              <span className="w-6 h-6 border-2 border-green-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" />
+            </div>
+          )}
+        </div>
+      )}
+    </div>
+  )
+}
+
 /* ─── Loading screen ────────────────────────────────────────── */
 function LoadingScreen() {
   const { tx } = useLang()

+/* ─── Location bottom sheet ─────────────────────────────────── */
+function LocationBottomSheet({
+  onSet,
+  onClose,
+}: {
+  onSet: (lat: number, lng: number, name: string) => void
+  onClose: () => void
+}) {
+  const [gpsStatus, setGpsStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'loading' | 'error'>('idle')
+  const [gpsError, setGpsError]   = useState('')
+
+  const handleGPS = () => {
+    if (!navigator.geolocation) {
+      setGpsError('GPS not supported on this device / GPS అందుబాటులో లేదు')
+      return
+    }
+    setGpsStatus('loading')
+    setGpsError('')
+    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
+      (pos) => {
+        const { latitude, longitude } = pos.coords
+        const name = nearestTown(latitude, longitude)
+        onSet(latitude, longitude, name)
+      },
+      (err) => {
+        setGpsStatus('error')
+        setGpsError(
+          err.code === 1
+            ? 'Location access denied. Search your town below. / లొకేషన్ అనుమతి లేదు. పట్టణం వెతకండి.'
+            : 'Could not get GPS. Search your town below. / GPS రాలేదు. పట్టణం వెతకండి.'
+        )
+      },
+      { timeout: 12000, enableHighAccuracy: true },
+    )
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/50 flex items-end justify-center">
+      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-4">
+        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
+          <div>
+            <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-lg leading-tight">
+              Where are you? / మీరు ఎక్కడ ఉన్నారు?
+            </h2>
+            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
+              See produce from farmers near you / దగ్గరలోని రైతుల పంట చూడండి
+            </p>
+          </div>
+          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-3xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
+        </div>
+
+        {/* GPS button */}
+        <button
+          onClick={handleGPS}
+          disabled={gpsStatus === 'loading'}
+          className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base active:bg-green-800 disabled:opacity-60"
+        >
+          {gpsStatus === 'loading' ? (
+            <>
+              <span className="animate-spin inline-block w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full" />
+              Detecting... / వెతుకుతోంది
+            </>
+          ) : (
+            <>📍 Use my current location / నా లొకేషన్ వాడండి</>
+          )}
+        </button>
+
+        {gpsError && (
+          <p className="text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 leading-snug">{gpsError}</p>
+        )}
+
+        {/* Divider */}
+        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
+          <div className="flex-1 border-t border-gray-200" />
+          <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-semibold">OR / లేదా</span>
+          <div className="flex-1 border-t border-gray-200" />
+        </div>
+
+        {/* Search input */}
+        <div>
+          <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">
+            Search your location / లొకేషన్ వెతకండి
+          </label>
+          <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mb-2">
+            Type any city, town or village in AP / Telangana<br />
+            ఆంధ్రప్రదేశ్ / తెలంగాణలో ఏ పట్టణమైనా టైప్ చేయండి
+          </p>
+          <LocationSearch
+            placeholder="e.g. Visakhapatnam, Hyderabad, Eluru..."
+            onSelect={(lat, lng, name) => onSet(lat, lng, name)}
+          />
+        </div>
+
+        <button
+          onClick={onClose}
+          className="w-full text-sm text-gray-500 py-2"
+        >
+          Skip for now / తర్వాత చేస్తాను
+        </button>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  )
+}

31	export function haversineKm(lat1: number, lng1: number, lat2: number, lng2: number): number {
32	  const R = 6371
33	  const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180
34	  const dLng = (lng2 - lng1) * Math.PI / 180
35	  const a =
36	    Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 +
37	    Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI / 180) *
38	    Math.sin(dLng / 2) ** 2
39	  return R * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a))
40	}
41	
42	export function nearestTown(lat: number, lng: number): string {
43	  let best = AP_TOWNS[0]
44	  let bestDist = haversineKm(lat, lng, best.lat, best.lng)
45	  for (const town of AP_TOWNS) {
46	    const d = haversineKm(lat, lng, town.lat, town.lng)
47	    if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; best = town }
48	  }
49	  return best.name
50	}
51	
52	export function formatDistance(km: number): string {
53	  if (km < 1) return `${Math.round(km * 1000)} m`
54	  if (km < 10) return `${km.toFixed(1)} km`
55	  return `${Math.round(km)} km`
56	}
57

1	'use client'
2	
3	import { useState } from 'react'
4	import Link from 'next/link'
5	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
6	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
7	import LanguageToggle from '@/components/LanguageToggle'
8	
9	type Order = {
10	  id: string
11	  produce_name: string | null
12	  quantity: number | null
13	  unit: string | null
14	  total_price: number | null
15	  pickup_location: string | null
16	  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'declined'
17	  created_at: string
18	  farmer?: {
19	    name: string
20	    slug: string
21	    village: string
22	  } | null
23	}
24	
25	export default function ConsumerOrdersPage() {
26	  const { tx } = useLang()
27	  const [phone, setPhone]     = useState('')
28	  const [orders, setOrders]   = useState<Order[]>([])
29	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
30	  const [searched, setSearched] = useState(false)
31	  const [error, setError]     = useState('')
32	
33	  const digits = phone.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10)
34	  const canSearch = digits.length === 10
35	
36	  const handleSearch = async () => {
37	    if (!canSearch) return
38	    setLoading(true)
39	    setError('')
40	    setSearched(false)
41	
42	    const { data, error: err } = await supabase
43	      .from('orders')
44	      .select('id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, pickup_location, status, created_at, farmer_id')
45	      .eq('buyer_phone', digits)
46	      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
47	      .limit(50)
48	
49	    if (err) {
50	      setError(err.message)
51	      setLoading(false)
52	      return
53	    }
54	
55	    // Fetch farmer names in one query
56	    const farmerIds = [...new Set((data ?? []).map((o) => o.farmer_id).filter(Boolean))]
57	    let farmerMap: Record<string, { name: string; slug: string; village: string }> = {}
58	
59	    if (farmerIds.length > 0) {
60	      const { data: farmers } = await supabase
61	        .from('farmers')
62	        .select('id, name, slug, village')
63	        .in('id', farmerIds)
64	      farmerMap = Object.fromEntries(
65	        (farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, { name: f.name, slug: f.slug, village: f.village }]),
66	      )
67	    }
68	
69	    const result: Order[] = (data ?? []).map((o) => ({
70	      ...o,
71	      farmer: farmerMap[o.farmer_id] ?? null,
72	    }))
73	
74	    setOrders(result)
75	    setSearched(true)
76	    setLoading(false)
77	  }
78	
79	  const statusColor = (s: string) =>
80	    s === 'approved'
81	      ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
82	      : s === 'declined'
83	      ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'
84	      : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'
85	
86	  const statusLabel = (s: string) =>
87	    s === 'approved' ? '✓ Confirmed' : s === 'declined' ? '✕ Declined' : '⏳ Pending'
88	
89	  return (
90	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16">
91	      {/* Header */}
92	      <div className="bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10">
93	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
94	          <Link href="/consumer" className="text-green-300 text-sm flex items-center gap-1">
95	            ← Back / వెనక్కు
96	          </Link>
97	          <LanguageToggle />
98	        </div>
99	        <h1 className="text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight">{tx.myOrders}</h1>
100	        <p className="text-green-400 text-sm mt-1">
101	          Your complete order history / మీ ఆర్డర్ల చరిత్ర
102	        </p>
103	      </div>
104	
105	      <div className="px-4 -mt-5 space-y-4 max-w-lg mx-auto">
106	        {/* Phone lookup card */}
107	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-3">
108	          <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 leading-snug">
109	            {tx.enterPhoneForOrders}
110	          </p>
111	          <div className="flex gap-2">
112	            <span className="flex items-center px-3 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-sm text-gray-600 font-medium whitespace-nowrap">
113	              +91
114	            </span>
115	            <input
116	              type="tel"
117	              inputMode="numeric"
118	              placeholder="9876543210"
119	              value={phone}
120	              onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
121	              onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleSearch()}
122	              className="flex-1 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
123	              maxLength={10}
124	            />
125	          </div>
126	          {error && (
127	            <p className="text-sm text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2">{error}</p>
128	          )}
129	          <button
130	            onClick={handleSearch}
131	            disabled={loading || !canSearch}
132	            className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base disabled:opacity-50 active:bg-green-800"
133	          >
134	            {loading ? 'Searching... / వెతుకుతోంది' : tx.lookupOrders}
135	          </button>
136	        </div>
137	
138	        {/* Results */}
139	        {searched && orders.length === 0 && (
140	          <div className="text-center py-14">
141	            <div className="text-5xl mb-3">📭</div>
142	            <p className="font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm">{tx.noOrdersYet}</p>
143	            <Link href="/consumer" className="mt-4 inline-block text-green-700 text-sm underline font-semibold">
144	              Browse produce → / పంట చూడండి
145	            </Link>
146	          </div>
147	        )}
148	
149	        {orders.length > 0 && (
150	          <div className="space-y-3">
151	            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
152	              {orders.length} order{orders.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} found
153	            </p>
154	            {orders.map((order) => (
155	              <div key={order.id} className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
156	                <div className="p-4 space-y-2">
157	                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
158	                    <div className="min-w-0">
159	                      <p className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-sm leading-tight">
160	                        {order.produce_name || '—'}
161	                      </p>
162	                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
163	                        {order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}
164	                        {order.total_price ? ` · ₹${order.total_price}` : ''}
165	                      </p>
166	                    </div>
167	                    <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap ${statusColor(order.status)}`}>
168	                      {statusLabel(order.status)}
169	                    </span>
170	                  </div>
171	
172	                  {order.farmer && (
173	                    <Link
174	                      href={`/farmer/${order.farmer.slug}`}
175	                      className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-green-700 font-semibold"
176	                    >
177	                      🧑‍🌾 {tx.orderedFrom} {order.farmer.name} · {order.farmer.village} ↗
178	                    </Link>
179	                  )}
180	
181	                  {order.pickup_location && (
182	                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">📍 {tx.pickedUpAt}: {order.pickup_location}</p>
183	                  )}
184	
185	                  <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-400">
186	                    {new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
187	                      day: 'numeric',
188	                      month: 'short',
189	                      year: 'numeric',
190	                      hour: '2-digit',
191	                      minute: '2-digit',
192	                    })}
193	                  </p>
194	                </div>
195	              </div>
196	            ))}
197	          </div>
198	        )}
199	      </div>
200	    </main>
201	  )
202	}
203

1	-- ================================================================
2	-- YFF Feature Migration
3	-- Run in: Supabase Dashboard → SQL Editor → New Query → Run
4	-- ================================================================
5	
6	-- 1. Harvest date on produce listings
7	ALTER TABLE produce_listings
8	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS harvest_date date;
9	
10	-- 2. Cover photo for farmer profile banner
11	ALTER TABLE farmers
12	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS cover_photo_url text;
13	
14	-- 3. Farmer avatar/profile photo
15	ALTER TABLE farmers
16	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS photo_url text;
17	
18	-- 4. Pesticide test certificate (photo URL)
19	ALTER TABLE farmers
20	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pesticide_cert_url text;
21	
22	-- 5. Pickup schedule: {days: string[], time_from: string, time_to: string}
23	ALTER TABLE farmers
24	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pickup_slots jsonb;
25	
26	-- 6. OTP table for secure farmer login
27	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS farmer_otps (
28	  id          uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
29	  phone       text        NOT NULL,
30	  otp         text        NOT NULL,
31	  expires_at  timestamptz NOT NULL,
32	  used        boolean     DEFAULT false,
33	  created_at  timestamptz DEFAULT now()
34	);
35	
36	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farmer_otps_phone   ON farmer_otps(phone);
37	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farmer_otps_expires ON farmer_otps(expires_at);
38	
39	-- RLS: only service-role key (used in API routes) can access
40	ALTER TABLE farmer_otps ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
41	
42	-- 7. Payment method and status on orders (COD support)
43	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS payment_method text DEFAULT 'cod';
44	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS payment_status text DEFAULT 'pending';
45	
46	-- 8. Farmer GPS location for distance-based discovery
47	ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS lat numeric(10,7);
48	ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS lng numeric(10,7);
49	ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS location_name text;
50

> DEVELOPER

how to see passwords in supabase

> AGENT

You can't see the original passwords — they're stored as one-way **scrypt hashes** in `farmers.password_hash`. That's intentional; even you as the admin can't reverse them.

To see what's stored, go to **Supabase Dashboard → Table Editor → farmers** and look at the `password_hash` column. You'll see values like:

```
a3f9b2c1d4e5...:7f8a9b0c1d2e3f4a5b6c7d8e9f0a1b2c...
```

That's `salt:hash` — not the original password.

**If a farmer forgets their password**, there's no way to recover it. The fix is to reset it: clear their `password_hash` to `null` in the Table Editor, and when they next log in with any password, the system will accept it and set that as their new password (that's the "first login after migration" path in `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts:59`).

To reset a farmer's password in Supabase SQL editor:
```sql
UPDATE farmers SET password_hash = NULL WHERE phone = '9876543210';
```

84	export default function ConsumerPage() {
85	  const [available, setAvailable]     = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
86	  const [comingSoon, setComingSoon]   = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
87	  const [filtered, setFiltered]       = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
88	  const [search, setSearch]           = useState('')
89	  const [method, setMethod]           = useState('all')
90	  const [category, setCategory]       = useState('all')
91	  const [loading, setLoading]         = useState(true)
92	  const [farmerCount, setFarmerCount] = useState(0)
93	
94	  // Consumer location
95	  const [consumerLat, setConsumerLat]           = useState<number | null>(null)
96	  const [consumerLng, setConsumerLng]           = useState<number | null>(null)
97	  const [consumerLocationName, setConsumerLocationName] = useState('')
98	  const [showLocationSheet, setShowLocationSheet]       = useState(false)
99	  const [distanceFilter, setDistanceFilter]             = useState<number | null>(null)
100	
101	  const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {
102	    setLoading(true)
103	    try {
104	      const [avRes, csRes] = await Promise.all([
105	        fetch('/api/produce', { cache: 'no-store' }),
106	        fetch('/api/produce?status=coming_soon', { cache: 'no-store' }),
107	      ])
108	      const av: ProduceListing[] = await avRes.json().catch(() => [])
109	      const cs: ProduceListing[] = await csRes.json().catch(() => [])
110	      const avArr = Array.isArray(av) ? av : []
111	      const csArr = Array.isArray(cs) ? cs : []
112	      setAvailable(avArr)
113	      setFiltered(avArr)
114	      setComingSoon(csArr)
115	      setFarmerCount(new Set(avArr.map((p) => p.farmer_id)).size)
116	    } catch { /* silent */ }
117	    setLoading(false)
118	  }, [])
119	
120	  useEffect(() => {
121	    fetchData()
122	    const onVisible = () => { if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') fetchData() }
123	    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible)
124	    const channel = supabase
125	      .channel('produce_listings_consumer')
126	      .on('postgres_changes', { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'produce_listings' }, fetchData)
127	      .subscribe()
128	    return () => {
129	      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible)
130	      supabase.removeChannel(channel)
131	    }
132	  }, [fetchData])
133	
134	  const doSearch = useCallback(async () => {
135	    if (!search && method === 'all' && category === 'all') {
136	      setFiltered(available)
137	      return
138	    }
139	    try {
140	      const p = new URLSearchParams()
141	      if (search)              p.set('q', search)
142	      if (method !== 'all')    p.set('method', method)
143	      if (category !== 'all')  p.set('category', category)
144	      const res = await fetch(`/api/produce/search?${p}`, { cache: 'no-store' })
145	      const data = await res.json().catch(() => [])
146	      setFiltered(Array.isArray(data) ? data : [])
147	    } catch { /* silent */ }
148	  }, [search, method, category, available])
149	
150	  useEffect(() => {
151	    const t = setTimeout(doSearch, 300)
152	    return () => clearTimeout(t)
153	  }, [doSearch])
154	
155	  // Load consumer location from localStorage
156	  useEffect(() => {
157	    const lat  = localStorage.getItem('yff_consumer_lat')
158	    const lng  = localStorage.getItem('yff_consumer_lng')
159	    const name = localStorage.getItem('yff_consumer_location_name')
160	    if (lat && lng) {
161	      setConsumerLat(Number(lat))
162	      setConsumerLng(Number(lng))
163	      setConsumerLocationName(name ?? '')
164	    } else if (!localStorage.getItem('yff_location_prompted')) {
165	      setShowLocationSheet(true)
166	    }
167	  }, [])
168	
169	  const saveConsumerLocation = useCallback((lat: number, lng: number, name: string) => {
170	    setConsumerLat(lat)
171	    setConsumerLng(lng)
172	    setConsumerLocationName(name)
173	    localStorage.setItem('yff_consumer_lat', String(lat))
174	    localStorage.setItem('yff_consumer_lng', String(lng))
175	    localStorage.setItem('yff_consumer_location_name', name)
176	    localStorage.setItem('yff_location_prompted', '1')
177	    setShowLocationSheet(false)
178	  }, [])
179	
180	  // Sort + distance-filter produce
181	  const displayItems = useMemo(() => {
182	    type WithDist = ProduceListing & { distKm: number | null }
183	    const withDist: WithDist[] = filtered.map((item) => ({
184	      ...item,
185	      distKm: (consumerLat && consumerLng && item.farmer?.lat && item.farmer?.lng)
186	        ? haversineKm(consumerLat, consumerLng, item.farmer.lat, item.farmer.lng)
187	        : null,
188	    }))
189	    if (!consumerLat || !consumerLng) return withDist
190	    let result = withDist
191	    if (distanceFilter) {
192	      result = result.filter((i) => i.distKm !== null && i.distKm <= distanceFilter)
193	    }
194	    return result.sort((a, b) => {
195	      if (a.distKm === null && b.distKm === null) return 0
196	      if (a.distKm === null) return 1
197	      if (b.distKm === null) return -1
198	      return a.distKm - b.distKm
199	    })
200	  }, [filtered, consumerLat, consumerLng, distanceFilter])
201	
202	  return (
203	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-12">
204	      <GlobalNav activeTab="consumer" />
205	
206	      {/* ── Hero ─────────────────────────────── */}
207	      <div className="bg-green-900">
208	        <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 pt-8 pb-14">
209	          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-extrabold text-white leading-snug">
210	            Fresh from your<br />local farmer
211	          </h1>
212	          <p className="text-green-300 text-base sm:text-lg mt-1 font-medium">
213	            మీ స్థానిక రైతు నుండి తాజా ఆహారం
214	          </p>
215	          <p className="text-green-400 text-sm mt-1">
216	            Straight from farm · No middlemen / నేరుగా పొలం నుండి · మధ్యవర్తులు లేరు
217	          </p>
218	
219	          {/* Stats */}
220	          <div className="flex gap-8 mt-6">
221	            {[
222	              { val: farmerCount,       en: 'Farmers',    te: 'రైతులు' },
223	              { val: available.length,  en: 'Products',   te: 'పంటలు' },
224	              { val: 0,                 en: 'Middlemen',  te: 'మధ్యవర్తులు' },
225	            ].map((s) => (
226	              <div key={s.en}>
227	                <div className="text-4xl font-black text-white">{s.val}</div>
228	                <div className="text-xs text-green-300 mt-0.5 leading-snug">
229	                  {s.en}<br />{s.te}
230	                </div>
231	              </div>
232	            ))}
233	          </div>
234	
235	          {/* Location pill */}
236	          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mt-5">
237	            <button
238	              onClick={() => setShowLocationSheet(true)}
239	              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-green-800 border border-green-700 text-green-200 text-sm font-semibold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full active:bg-green-700"
240	            >
241	              📍 {consumerLocationName || 'Set location / లొకేషన్ పెట్టండి'}
242	              <span className="text-green-400 text-xs ml-0.5">✎</span>
243	            </button>
244	            <Link
245	              href="/consumer/orders"
246	              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-green-800 border border-green-700 text-green-200 text-xs font-semibold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full"
247	            >
248	              📦 My Orders / నా ఆర్డర్లు →
249	            </Link>
250	          </div>
251	        </div>
252	      </div>
253	
254	      {/* ── Search card (floats over hero) ────── */}
255	      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 -mt-7">
256	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-4 space-y-3">
257	          <div className="relative">
258	            <svg className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5">
259	              <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"/><path d="m21 21-4.35-4.35" strokeLinecap="round"/>
260	            </svg>
261	            <input
262	              type="search"
263	              placeholder="Search produce... / పంట వెతకండి..."
264	              value={search}
265	              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
266	              className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 border-2 border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
267	            />
268	          </div>
269	          <select
270	            value={method}
271	            onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value)}
272	            className="w-full px-4 py-3 border-2 border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-base bg-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
273	          >
274	            <option value="all">🌿 All methods / అన్ని పద్ధతులు</option>
275	            <option value="natural">🌱 Natural / సహజం</option>
276	            <option value="organic">🍃 Organic / సేంద్రీయ</option>
277	            <option value="low_chemical">⚡ Low chemical / తక్కువ రసాయన</option>
278	          </select>
279	        </div>
280	      </div>
281	
282	      {/* ── Category chips ───────────────────── */}
283	      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto mt-4 px-4">
284	        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide pb-1">
285	          {CATEGORIES.map((chip) => (
286	            <button
287	              key={chip.key}
288	              onClick={() => setCategory(chip.key)}
289	              className={`flex-shrink-0 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full text-sm font-semibold border transition-all ${
290	                category === chip.key
291	                  ? 'bg-green-700 text-white border-green-700'
292	                  : 'bg-white text-gray-700 border-gray-200'
293	              }`}
294	            >
295	              {chip.en} / {chip.te}
296	            </button>
297	          ))}
298	        </div>
299	      </div>
300	
301	      {/* ── Distance filter chips (only when location set) ── */}
302	      {consumerLat && consumerLng && (
303	        <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto mt-3 px-4">
304	          <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide pb-1">
305	            {([null, 5, 10, 25] as const).map((d) => (
306	              <button
307	                key={d ?? 'all'}
308	                onClick={() => setDistanceFilter(d)}
309	                className={`flex-shrink-0 px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-sm font-semibold border transition-all ${
310	                  distanceFilter === d
311	                    ? 'bg-green-700 text-white border-green-700'
312	                    : 'bg-white text-gray-700 border-gray-200'
313	                }`}
314	              >
315	                {d === null ? 'All / అన్నీ' : `< ${d} km`}
316	              </button>
317	            ))}
318	          </div>
319	        </div>
320	      )}
321	
322	      {/* ── Available now ────────────────────── */}
323	      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 mt-6">
324	        <div className="flex items-end justify-between mb-4">
325	          <div>
326	            <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-900">Available now / ఇప్పుడు అందుబాటులో</h2>
327	            <p className="text-green-700 font-semibold text-sm leading-tight">
328	              Fresh produce from farms nearby / తాజా పంట
329	            </p>
330	          </div>
331	          {!loading && (
332	            <span className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-400">
333	              {filtered.length} items / వస్తువులు
334	            </span>
335	          )}
336	        </div>
337	
338	        {loading ? (
339	          <LoadingSkeleton />
340	        ) : displayItems.length === 0 ? (
341	          distanceFilter ? (
342	            <DistanceEmptyState km={distanceFilter} onClear={() => setDistanceFilter(null)} />
343	          ) : (
344	            <EmptyState />
345	          )
346	        ) : (
347	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
348	            {displayItems.map((item) => (
349	              <ProduceCard key={item.id} item={item} distanceKm={'distKm' in item ? (item as ProduceListing & { distKm: number | null }).distKm : null} />
350	            ))}
351	          </div>
352	        )}
353	      </div>
354	
355	      {/* ── Coming soon ──────────────────────── */}
356	      {!loading && comingSoon.length > 0 && (
357	        <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 mt-10">
358	          <div className="mb-4">
359	            <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-900">Coming soon / త్వరలో వస్తుంది</h2>
360	            <p className="text-amber-600 font-semibold text-sm">
361	              Reserve early / ముందుగానే రిజర్వ్
362	            </p>
363	          </div>
364	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
365	            {comingSoon.map((item) => (
366	              <ComingSoonCard key={item.id} item={item} />
367	            ))}
368	          </div>
369	        </div>
370	      )}
371	
372	      {/* ── Demand intent banner ─────────────── */}
373	      <DemandIntentBanner />
374	
375	      {/* ── Floating cart button ─────────────── */}
376	      <CartFab />
377	
378	      {/* ── Location bottom sheet ─────────────── */}
379	      {showLocationSheet && (
380	        <LocationBottomSheet
381	          onSet={saveConsumerLocation}
382	          onClose={() => {
383	            localStorage.setItem('yff_location_prompted', '1')
384	            setShowLocationSheet(false)
385	          }}
386	        />
387	      )}
388	    </main>
389	  )
390	}
391	
392	/* ─── Produce card ──────────────────────────────────────── */
393	function ProduceCard({ item, distanceKm }: { item: ProduceListing; distanceKm?: number | null }) {
394	  const emoji      = item.emoji ?? '🌿'
395	  const emojiBg    = EMOJI_BG[emoji] ?? 'bg-green-50'
396	  const method     = item.method?.toLowerCase() ?? 'natural'
397	  const badge      = METHOD_STYLE[method]  ?? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
398	  const badgeLabel = METHOD_LABEL[method] ?? 'Natural'
399	  const farmer     = item.farmer
400	  const farmerHref = farmer ? `/farmer/${farmer.slug}` : '#'
401	  const unit       = item.unit || 'kg'
402	
403	  const { cart, addItem, setQty } = useCart()
404	  const inCart = cart[item.id]
405	
406	  const canAdd = !!farmer && !!farmer.phone
407	
408	  const [liveStock, setLiveStock] = useState<number | null>(item.stock_qty ?? null)
409	  const [stockMsg, setStockMsg]   = useState('')
410	  const [adding, setAdding]       = useState(false)
411	
412	  useEffect(() => { setLiveStock(item.stock_qty ?? null) }, [item.stock_qty])
413	
414	  const isOutOfStock = liveStock !== null && liveStock <= 0
415	  const atMax        = liveStock !== null && inCart != null && inCart.qty >= liveStock
416	
417	  const handleAdd = async () => {
418	    if (!farmer) return
419	    setAdding(true)
420	    setStockMsg('')
421	
422	    const { data } = await supabase
423	      .from('produce_listings')
424	      .select('stock_qty')
425	      .eq('id', item.id)
426	      .single()
427	
428	    const fresh = data?.stock_qty ?? null
429	    if (fresh !== null) setLiveStock(fresh)
430	    setAdding(false)
431	
432	    if (fresh !== null) {
433	      if (fresh <= 0) {
434	        setStockMsg('Out of stock / అయిపోయింది')
435	        return
436	      }
437	      const curQty = inCart?.qty ?? 0
438	      if (curQty >= fresh) {
439	        setStockMsg(`Maximum available / గరిష్ట పరిమాణం: ${fresh} ${unit}`)
440	        return
441	      }
442	      if (curQty + 1 > fresh) {
443	        setStockMsg(`Stock updated / స్టాక్ మారింది, only ${fresh} ${unit} available`)
444	        return
445	      }
446	    }
447	
448	    addItem({
449	      listingId: item.id,
450	      name: item.name,
451	      variety: item.variety,
452	      emoji: item.emoji,
453	      unit,
454	      stockQty: fresh != null ? fresh : (item.stock_qty ?? undefined),
455	      pricePerKg: item.price_tier_1_price,
456	      priceTier1Qty: item.price_tier_1_qty,
457	      priceTier1Price: item.price_tier_1_price,
458	      priceTier2Qty: item.price_tier_2_qty,
459	      priceTier2Price: item.price_tier_2_price,
460	      priceTier3Price: item.price_tier_3_price,
461	      farmerId: farmer.id,
462	      farmerName: farmer.name,
463	      farmerPhone: farmer.phone,
464	      farmerVillage: farmer.village,
465	      farmerSlug: farmer.slug,
466	      farmerPickupLocations: farmer.pickup_locations ?? [],
467	      farmerPickupSlots: farmer.pickup_slots ?? null,
468	    }, 1)
469	  }
470	
471	  const handleInc = () => {
472	    if (liveStock !== null && inCart.qty >= liveStock) {
473	      setStockMsg(`Maximum available / గరిష్ట పరిమాణం: ${liveStock} ${unit}`)
474	      return
475	    }
476	    setStockMsg('')
477	    setQty(item.id, inCart.qty + 1)
478	  }
479	
480	  return (
481	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col">
482	      {/* Image (or emoji fallback) */}
483	      <Link href={farmerHref}>
484	        {item.image_url ? (
485	          <div className="bg-gray-100 aspect-[4/3] overflow-hidden">
486	            <img
487	              src={item.image_url}
488	              alt={item.name}
489	              loading="lazy"
490	              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
491	            />
492	          </div>
493	        ) : (
494	          <div className={`${emojiBg} flex items-center justify-center py-7`}>
495	            <span className="text-5xl">{emoji}</span>
496	          </div>
497	        )}
498	      </Link>
499	
500	      <div className="p-3 flex flex-col flex-1 gap-1">
501	        {/* Name + variety */}
502	        <Link href={farmerHref}>
503	          <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">{item.name}</h3>
504	          {item.variety && (
505	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{item.variety}</p>
506	          )}
507	        </Link>
508	
509	        {/* Farmer info */}
510	        {farmer && (
511	          <Link href={farmerHref} className="text-xs text-gray-500 leading-snug">
512	            🧑‍🌾 {farmer.name}<br />
513	            📍 {farmer.village}
514	          </Link>
515	        )}
516	
517	        {/* Distance badge */}
518	        {distanceKm != null && (
519	          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full self-start">
520	            📍 {formatDistance(distanceKm)} away
521	          </span>
522	        )}
523	
524	        {/* Price + method badge */}
525	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-1">
526	          <span className="text-green-700 font-black text-lg">
527	            {item.price_tier_1_price ? `₹${item.price_tier_1_price}` : '—'}
528	            <span className="text-xs font-normal text-gray-400">/{unit}</span>
529	          </span>
530	          <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${badge}`}>
531	            {badgeLabel}
532	          </span>
533	        </div>
534	
535	        {/* Freshness badge */}
536	        {item.harvest_date && <FreshnessBadge harvestDate={item.harvest_date} />}
537	
538	        {/* Stock display */}
539	        {liveStock != null && (
540	          <p className={`text-xs font-medium ${liveStock === 0 ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>
541	            {liveStock === 0
542	              ? 'Out of stock / అయిపోయింది'
543	              : `${liveStock} ${unit} left / మిగిలాయి`}
544	          </p>
545	        )}
546	
547	        {/* Stock warning */}
548	        {stockMsg && (
549	          <p className="text-xs text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 leading-snug text-center">
550	            {stockMsg}
551	          </p>
552	        )}
553	
554	        {/* CTA */}
555	        {!canAdd ? (
556	          <Link
557	            href={farmerHref}
558	            className="mt-2 block text-center w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
559	          >
560	            View / చూడండి
561	          </Link>
562	        ) : isOutOfStock ? (
563	          <button
564	            disabled
565	            className="mt-2 w-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm cursor-not-allowed"
566	          >
567	            Out of stock / అయిపోయింది
568	          </button>
569	        ) : !inCart ? (
570	          <button
571	            onClick={handleAdd}
572	            disabled={adding}
573	            className="mt-2 w-full bg-green-700 active:bg-green-800 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm disabled:opacity-60"
574	          >
575	            {adding ? 'Checking... / తనిఖీ' : '+ Add to cart / చేర్చు'}
576	          </button>
577	        ) : (
578	          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-xl px-2 py-1.5">
579	            <button
580	              onClick={() => { setQty(item.id, inCart.qty - 1); setStockMsg('') }}
581	              className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white border border-green-300 text-green-800 text-lg font-bold"
582	              aria-label="Decrease"
583	            >
584	              −
585	            </button>
586	            <span className="font-extrabold text-green-900 text-sm">
587	              {inCart.qty} {unit}
588	            </span>
589	            <button
590	              onClick={handleInc}
591	              disabled={atMax}
592	              className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-lg text-lg font-bold transition-colors ${
593	                atMax
594	                  ? 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-400 cursor-not-allowed'
595	                  : 'bg-green-700 text-white'
596	              }`}
597	              aria-label="Increase"
598	            >
599	              +
600	            </button>
601	          </div>
602	        )}
603	      </div>
604	    </div>
605	  )
606	}
607	
608	/* ─── Coming soon card ──────────────────────────────────── */
609	function ComingSoonCard({ item }: { item: ProduceListing }) {
610	  const emoji   = item.emoji ?? '🌱'
611	  const emojiBg = EMOJI_BG[emoji] ?? 'bg-amber-50'
612	  const farmer  = item.farmer
613	
614	  return (
615	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border-2 border-dashed border-amber-200 flex flex-col">
616	      <div className={`${emojiBg} flex items-center justify-center py-7`}>
617	        <span className="text-5xl">{emoji}</span>
618	      </div>
619	      <div className="p-3 flex flex-col flex-1 gap-1">
620	        <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-700 text-base leading-tight">{item.name}</h3>
621	        {farmer && (
622	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">🧑‍🌾 {farmer.name}</p>
623	        )}
624	        {item.available_to && (
625	          <p className="text-xs text-amber-600 font-medium">
626	            Ready / సిద్ధం: {new Date(item.available_to).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}
627	          </p>
628	        )}
629	        <span className="mt-1 inline-block bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full self-start">
630	          Coming soon / త్వరలో
631	        </span>
632	        {farmer && (
633	          <Link
634	            href={`/farmer/${farmer.slug}`}
635	            className="mt-2 block text-center w-full border-2 border-green-600 text-green-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
636	          >
637	            View farmer / రైతు చూడండి
638	          </Link>
639	        )}
640	      </div>
641	    </div>
642	  )
643	}
644	
645	/* ─── Loading skeleton ──────────────────────────────────── */
646	function LoadingSkeleton() {
647	  return (
648	    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
649	      {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
650	        <div key={i} className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden animate-pulse">
651	          <div className="h-28 bg-gray-100" />
652	          <div className="p-3 space-y-2">
653	            <div className="h-4 bg-gray-100 rounded w-3/4" />
654	            <div className="h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded w-1/2" />
655	            <div className="h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded w-2/3" />
656	            <div className="h-11 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl mt-3" />
657	          </div>
658	        </div>
659	      ))}
660	    </div>
661	  )
662	}
663	
664	/* ─── Distance empty state ──────────────────────────────── */
665	function DistanceEmptyState({ km, onClear }: { km: number; onClear: () => void }) {
666	  return (
667	    <div className="text-center py-14">
668	      <div className="text-6xl mb-4">📍</div>
669	      <p className="text-gray-800 font-bold text-lg">No farmers within {km} km</p>
670	      <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-1">
671	        {km} కి.మీ లోపల రైతులు లేరు
672	      </p>
673	      <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs mt-2 leading-snug px-8">
674	        Farmers nearby may not have set their location yet.<br />
675	        దగ్గరలోని రైతులు ఇంకా లొకేషన్ పెట్టలేదు.
676	      </p>
677	      <button
678	        onClick={onClear}
679	        className="mt-5 bg-green-700 text-white font-bold px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
680	      >
681	        Show all produce / అన్ని పంటలు చూపించు
682	      </button>
683	    </div>
684	  )
685	}
686	
687	/* ─── Empty state ───────────────────────────────────────── */
688	function EmptyState() {
689	  return (
690	    <div className="text-center py-14">
691	      <div className="text-6xl mb-4">🔍</div>
692	      <p className="text-gray-800 font-bold text-lg">No produce found / పంట కనుగొనబడలేదు</p>
693	      <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-2">
694	        Try a different search or category<br />
695	        వేరే వెతకండి లేదా వేరే వర్గాన్ని ఎంచుకోండి
696	      </p>
697	    </div>
698	  )
699	}
700	
701	/* ─── Location bottom sheet ─────────────────────────────────── */
702	function LocationBottomSheet({
703	  onSet,
704	  onClose,
705	}: {
706	  onSet: (lat: number, lng: number, name: string) => void
707	  onClose: () => void
708	}) {
709	  const [gpsStatus, setGpsStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'loading' | 'error'>('idle')
710	  const [gpsError, setGpsError]   = useState('')
711	
712	  const handleGPS = () => {
713	    if (!navigator.geolocation) {
714	      setGpsError('GPS not supported on this device / GPS అందుబాటులో లేదు')
715	      return
716	    }
717	    setGpsStatus('loading')
718	    setGpsError('')
719	    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
720	      (pos) => {

35	export type CartState = Record<string, CartItem>
36	
37	export type ConsumerInfo = { name: string; phone: string }
38	
39	const CART_KEY = 'yff_cart_v1'
40	const CONSUMER_KEY = 'REDACTED'
41	const CART_EVENT = 'yff:cart-change'
42	
43	const readCart = (): CartState => {
44	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return {}
45	  try {
46	    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CART_KEY)
47	    return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as CartState) : {}
48	  } catch {
49	    return {}
50	  }
51	}
52	
53	const writeCart = (next: CartState) => {
54	  localStorage.setItem(CART_KEY, JSON.stringify(next))
55	  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(CART_EVENT))
56	}
57	
58	function getActiveTier(qty: number, item: CartItem): { price?: number; isDiscount: boolean } {
59	  const { priceTier1Qty, priceTier1Price, priceTier2Qty, priceTier2Price, priceTier3Price } = item
60	  if (priceTier1Qty == null || priceTier1Price == null)
61	    return { price: item.pricePerKg, isDiscount: false }
62	  if (qty <= priceTier1Qty)
63	    return { price: priceTier1Price, isDiscount: false }
64	  if (priceTier2Qty != null && priceTier2Price != null) {
65	    if (qty <= priceTier2Qty) return { price: priceTier2Price, isDiscount: true }
66	    return { price: priceTier3Price ?? priceTier2Price, isDiscount: true }
67	  }
68	  if (priceTier3Price != null) return { price: priceTier3Price, isDiscount: true }
69	  return { price: priceTier1Price, isDiscount: false }
70	}
71	
72	export function useCart() {
73	  const [cart, setCart] = useState<CartState>({})
74	
75	  useEffect(() => {
76	    setCart(readCart())
77	    const sync = () => setCart(readCart())
78	    window.addEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync)
79	    window.addEventListener('storage', sync)
80	    return () => {
81	      window.removeEventListener(CART_EVENT, sync)
82	      window.removeEventListener('storage', sync)
83	    }
84	  }, [])
85	
86	  const addItem = useCallback((item: Omit<CartItem, 'qty'>, qty = 1) => {
87	    const next = { ...readCart() }
88	    const existing = next[item.listingId]
89	    const rawQty = Math.max(1, (existing?.qty ?? 0) + qty)
90	    const newQty = item.stockQty != null ? Math.min(rawQty, item.stockQty) : rawQty
91	    const merged = { ...item, qty: newQty }
92	    const { price } = getActiveTier(newQty, merged)
93	    next[item.listingId] = { ...merged, pricePerKg: price ?? item.pricePerKg }
94	    writeCart(next)
95	  }, [])
96	
97	  const setQty = useCallback((listingId: string, qty: number) => {
98	    const next = { ...readCart() }
99	    if (qty <= 0) delete next[listingId]
100	    else if (next[listingId]) {
101	      const existing = next[listingId]
102	      const cappedQty = existing.stockQty != null ? Math.min(qty, existing.stockQty) : qty
103	      const updated = { ...existing, qty: cappedQty }
104	      const { price } = getActiveTier(cappedQty, updated)
105	      next[listingId] = { ...updated, pricePerKg: price ?? updated.pricePerKg }
106	    }
107	    writeCart(next)
108	  }, [])
109	
110	  const removeItem = useCallback((listingId: string) => {
111	    const next = { ...readCart() }
112	    delete next[listingId]
113	    writeCart(next)
114	  }, [])
115	
116	  const clear = useCallback(() => writeCart({}), [])
117	
118	  const clearFarmer = useCallback((farmerId: string) => {
119	    const next = { ...readCart() }
120	    for (const key of Object.keys(next)) {
121	      if (next[key].farmerId === farmerId) delete next[key]
122	    }
123	    writeCart(next)
124	  }, [])
125	
126	  const items = Object.values(cart)
127	  const count = items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.qty, 0)
128	
129	  return { cart, items, count, addItem, setQty, removeItem, clear, clearFarmer }
130	}
131	
132	export function useConsumerInfo() {
133	  const [info, setInfo] = useState<ConsumerInfo>({ name: '', phone: '' })
134	
135	  useEffect(() => {
136	    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return
137	    try {
138	      const raw = localStorage.getItem(CONSUMER_KEY)
139	      if (raw) setInfo(JSON.parse(raw))
140	    } catch { /* ignore */ }
141	  }, [])
142	
143	  const save = useCallback((next: ConsumerInfo) => {
144	    setInfo(next)
145	    localStorage.setItem(CONSUMER_KEY, JSON.stringify(next))
146	  }, [])
147	
148	  return { info, save }
149	}

554	function QtyStepper({
555	  qty,
556	  onDec,
557	  onInc,
558	  maxQty,
559	}: {
560	  qty: number
561	  onDec: () => void
562	  onInc: () => void
563	  maxQty?: number
564	}) {
565	  const atMax = maxQty != null && qty >= maxQty
566	  return (
567	    <div className="flex items-center border border-gray-200 rounded-full">
568	      <button
569	        onClick={onDec}
570	        className="w-8 h-8 text-lg text-gray-700 flex items-center justify-center active:bg-gray-100 rounded-l-full"
571	        aria-label="Decrease"
572	      >
573	        −
574	      </button>
575	      <span className="w-10 text-center font-bold text-sm text-gray-900">
576	        {qty}
577	      </span>
578	      <button
579	        onClick={onInc}
580	        disabled={atMax}
581	        className={`w-8 h-8 text-lg flex items-center justify-center rounded-r-full ${
582	          atMax
583	            ? 'text-gray-300 cursor-not-allowed'
584	            : 'text-gray-700 active:bg-gray-100'
585	        }`}
586	        aria-label="Increase"
587	      >
588	        +
589	      </button>
590	    </div>
591	  )
592	}
593

595	  return (

597	      <circle cx="9" cy="21" r="1" />
598	      <circle cx="20" cy="21" r="1" />

600	    </svg>
601	  )
602	}

100	const EMOJI_OPTIONS = ['🍅', '🍌', '🥭', '🫑', '🥬', '🍆', '🥕', '🌽', '🧅', '🧄', '🥦', '🌿', '🍓', '🫒', '🌾', '🥥']

105	export default function FarmerDashboard() {
106	  const router = useRouter()
107	  const { tx } = useLang()
108	  const [farmer, setFarmer] = useState<Farmer | null>(null)
109	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
110	  const [notFound, setNotFound] = useState(false)
111	  const [activeListings, setActiveListings] = useState(0)
112	  const [pendingOrders, setPendingOrders] = useState<Order[]>([])
113	  const [approvedCount, setApprovedCount] = useState(0)
114	  const [totalRevenue, setTotalRevenue] = useState(0)
115	  const [ordersFilter, setOrdersFilter] = useState<'today' | 'week' | 'month'>('week')
116	  const [processingOrderId, setProcessingOrderId] = useState<string | null>(null)
117	  const [processingPaidId, setProcessingPaidId] = useState<string | null>(null)
118	  const [demandBars, setDemandBars] = useState<DemandBar[]>([])
119	  const [monthlyRevenue, setMonthlyRevenue] = useState(0)
120	  const [monthlyOrderCount, setMonthlyOrderCount] = useState(0)
121	  const [weeklyEarnings, setWeeklyEarnings] = useState<number[]>([0, 0, 0, 0])
122	  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
123	  const [showProfileEdit, setShowProfileEdit] = useState(false)
124	  const [showListings, setShowListings] = useState(false)
125	
126	  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
127	    const farmerId = localStorage.getItem('yff_farmer_id')
128	    if (!farmerId) { router.replace('/farmer/login'); return }
129	
130	    const { data: farmerData } = await supabase
131	      .from('farmers')
132	      .select('*')
133	      .eq('id', farmerId)
134	      .maybeSingle()
135	
136	    if (!farmerData) { setNotFound(true); setLoading(false); return }
137	    setFarmer(farmerData)
138	
139	    const monthStart = new Date()
140	    monthStart.setDate(1)
141	    monthStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
142	
143	    const [listingsRes, pendingRes, approvedRes, intentsRes, monthlyRes] = await Promise.all([
144	      supabase.from('produce_listings').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'available'),
145	      supabase.from('orders').select('*').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'pending').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
146	      supabase.from('orders').select('id, total_price').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'approved').gte('created_at', new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString()),
147	      supabase.from('demand_intents').select('crop_name, quantity_kg').eq('region_slug', farmerData.region_slug).eq('fulfilled', false),
148	      supabase.from('orders').select('id, total_price, created_at').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'approved').gte('created_at', monthStart.toISOString()),
149	    ])
150	
151	    setActiveListings(listingsRes.count ?? 0)
152	    setPendingOrders((pendingRes.data ?? []) as Order[])
153	    const approved = approvedRes.data ?? []
154	    setApprovedCount(approved.length)
155	    setTotalRevenue(approved.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0))
156	
157	    // Monthly earnings
158	    const monthly = monthlyRes.data ?? []
159	    setMonthlyRevenue(monthly.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0))
160	    setMonthlyOrderCount(monthly.length)
161	
162	    // Break into 4 weekly buckets (days 1-7, 8-14, 15-21, 22+)
163	    const weeks = [0, 0, 0, 0]
164	    for (const o of monthly) {
165	      const day = new Date(o.created_at).getDate()
166	      const bucket = day <= 7 ? 0 : day <= 14 ? 1 : day <= 21 ? 2 : 3
167	      weeks[bucket] += o.total_price ?? 0
168	    }
169	    setWeeklyEarnings(weeks)
170	
171	    const map: Record<string, number> = {}
172	    for (const row of intentsRes.data ?? []) {
173	      map[row.crop_name] = (map[row.crop_name] ?? 0) + (Number(row.quantity_kg) || 0)
174	    }
175	    setDemandBars(
176	      Object.entries(map)
177	        .map(([crop_name, total_qty]) => ({ crop_name, total_qty }))
178	        .sort((a, b) => b.total_qty - a.total_qty)
179	        .slice(0, 5)
180	    )
181	
182	    setLoading(false)
183	  }, [router])
184	
185	  useEffect(() => { loadDashboard() }, [loadDashboard])
186	
187	  // Auto-open the profile edit modal the first time an incomplete farmer lands here.
188	  useEffect(() => {
189	    if (!loading && farmer && !isProfileComplete(farmer)) {
190	      setShowProfileEdit(true)
191	    }
192	  }, [loading, farmer])
193	
194	  const handleLogout = () => {
195	    localStorage.removeItem('yff_farmer_id')
196	    localStorage.removeItem('yff_farmer_slug')
197	    router.replace('/farmer/login')
198	  }
199	
200	  const handleApprove = async (orderId: string) => {
201	    setProcessingOrderId(orderId)
202	    await supabase.from('orders').update({ status: 'approved' }).eq('id', orderId)
203	    setPendingOrders((prev) => prev.filter((o) => o.id !== orderId))
204	    setApprovedCount((c) => c + 1)
205	    setProcessingOrderId(null)
206	  }
207	
208	  const handleDecline = async (orderId: string) => {
209	    setProcessingOrderId(orderId)
210	    await supabase.from('orders').update({ status: 'declined' }).eq('id', orderId)
211	    setPendingOrders((prev) => prev.filter((o) => o.id !== orderId))
212	    setProcessingOrderId(null)
213	  }
214	
215	  const handleMarkPaid = async (orderId: string) => {
216	    setProcessingPaidId(orderId)
217	    await supabase.from('orders').update({ payment_status: 'completed' }).eq('id', orderId)
218	    setPendingOrders((prev) =>
219	      prev.map((o) => o.id === orderId ? { ...o, payment_status: 'completed' } : o)
220	    )
221	    setProcessingPaidId(null)
222	  }

228	      const todayStart = new Date()
229	      todayStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
230	      return t >= todayStart.getTime()
231	    }
232	    if (ordersFilter === 'week') return t >= now - 7 * 86400000
233	    return t >= now - 30 * 86400000
234	  })
235

237	  if (notFound) return <FarmerNotFound onLogout={handleLogout} />
238

492	/* ─── Profile edit modal ─────────────────────────────────── */
493	function ProfileEditModal({
494	  farmer,
495	  onClose,
496	  onSaved,
497	}: {
498	  farmer: Farmer
499	  onClose: () => void
500	  onSaved: (updated: Farmer) => void
501	}) {
502	  const { tx } = useLang()
503	  const [name, setName] = useState(farmer.name ?? '')
504	  const [village, setVillage] = useState(farmer.village ?? '')
505	  const [district, setDistrict] = useState(farmer.district ?? '')
506	  const [method, setMethod] = useState(farmer.method ?? 'natural')
507	  const [sinceYear, setSinceYear] = useState(
508	    farmer.farming_since_year ? String(farmer.farming_since_year) : '',
509	  )
510	  const [pickupLocations, setPickupLocations] = useState<string[]>(
511	    Array.isArray(farmer.pickup_locations) ? farmer.pickup_locations : [],
512	  )
513	  const [newPickup, setNewPickup] = useState('')
514	
515	  // Cover photo
516	  const [coverFile, setCoverFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
517	  const [coverPreview, setCoverPreview] = useState('')
518	  const [existingCoverUrl, setExistingCoverUrl] = useState(farmer.cover_photo_url ?? '')
519	
520	  // Avatar photo
521	  const [avatarFile, setAvatarFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
522	  const [avatarPreview, setAvatarPreview] = useState('')
523	  const [existingAvatarUrl, setExistingAvatarUrl] = useState(farmer.photo_url ?? '')
524	
525	  // Pesticide cert
526	  const [certFile, setCertFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
527	  const [certPreview, setCertPreview] = useState('')
528	  const [existingCertUrl, setExistingCertUrl] = useState(farmer.pesticide_cert_url ?? '')
529	
530	  // Pickup schedule
531	  const ALL_DAYS = ['Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday', 'Sunday']
532	  const [slotDays, setSlotDays] = useState<string[]>(farmer.pickup_slots?.days ?? [])
533	  const [slotFrom, setSlotFrom] = useState(farmer.pickup_slots?.time_from ?? '08:00')
534	  const [slotTo, setSlotTo]   = useState(farmer.pickup_slots?.time_to   ?? '12:00')
535	
536	  // Farm GPS location
537	  const [farmerLat, setFarmerLat] = useState<number | null>(farmer.lat ?? null)
538	  const [farmerLng, setFarmerLng] = useState<number | null>(farmer.lng ?? null)
539	  const [farmerLocationName, setFarmerLocationName] = useState(farmer.location_name ?? '')
540	  const [locating, setLocating] = useState(false)
541	  const [locError, setLocError] = useState('')
542	
543	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
544	  const [error, setError] = useState('')
545	
546	  const handleFarmerGPS = () => {
547	    if (!navigator.geolocation) {
548	      setLocError('Geolocation not supported on this device.')
549	      return
550	    }
551	    setLocating(true)
552	    setLocError('')
553	    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
554	      (pos) => {
555	        const { latitude, longitude } = pos.coords
556	        setFarmerLat(latitude)
557	        setFarmerLng(longitude)
558	        // Use village name as display name since we have it
559	        setFarmerLocationName(farmer.village || 'Farm')
560	        setLocating(false)
561	      },
562	      (err) => {
563	        setLocError(
564	          err.code === 1
565	            ? 'Location permission denied / లొకేషన్ అనుమతి లేదు. Please allow location in browser settings.'
566	            : 'Could not get location. Please try again. / మళ్ళీ ప్రయత్నించండి.'
567	        )
568	        setLocating(false)
569	      },
570	      { timeout: 15000, enableHighAccuracy: true },
571	    )
572	  }
573	
574	  const handlePickFile = (
575	    e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
576	    setFile: (f: File | null) => void,
577	    setPreview: (s: string) => void,
578	    currentPreview: string,
579	  ) => {
580	    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
581	    if (!file) return
582	    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) { setError(tx.pickImageFile); return }
583	    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { setError(tx.imageTooLarge); return }
584	    setError('')
585	    if (currentPreview) URL.revokeObjectURL(currentPreview)
586	    setFile(file)
587	    setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(file))
588	  }
589	
590	  const uploadProfileImage = async (file: File, pathSuffix: string): Promise<string | null> => {
591	    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'
592	    const path = `${farmer.id}/${pathSuffix}.${ext}`
593	    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage
594	      .from('farm-images')
595	      .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: true })
596	    if (upErr) { setError(`Upload failed: ${upErr.message}`); return null }
597	    const { data } = supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path)
598	    return data.publicUrl
599	  }
600	
601	  const toggleDay = (day: string) => {
602	    setSlotDays((prev) =>
603	      prev.includes(day) ? prev.filter((d) => d !== day) : [...prev, day],
604	    )
605	  }
606	
607	  const addPickup = () => {
608	    const v = newPickup.trim()
609	    if (!v) return
610	    if (pickupLocations.includes(v)) { setNewPickup(''); return }
611	    setPickupLocations((prev) => [...prev, v])
612	    setNewPickup('')
613	  }
614	  const removePickup = (loc: string) =>
615	    setPickupLocations((prev) => prev.filter((l) => l !== loc))
616	
617	  const handleSave = async () => {
618	    if (!name.trim()) { setError(tx.nameRequired); return }
619	    if (!village.trim()) { setError(tx.villageRequired); return }
620	    setLoading(true)
621	    setError('')
622	
623	    // Build unique slug from name if current slug is still auto-generated
624	    const isAutoSlug = /^f-\d{10}-[a-z0-9]{4}$/.test(farmer.slug ?? '')
625	    const baseSlug = name
626	      .toLowerCase()
627	      .trim()
628	      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
629	      .replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '')
630	      .slice(0, 40) || 'farmer'
631	    let newSlug = farmer.slug
632	    if (isAutoSlug && baseSlug) {
633	      const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5)
634	      newSlug = `${baseSlug}-${rand}`
635	    }
636	
637	    // Upload photos in parallel
638	    const [newCoverUrl, newAvatarUrl, newCertUrl] = await Promise.all([
639	      coverFile ? uploadProfileImage(coverFile, 'cover') : Promise.resolve(null),
640	      avatarFile ? uploadProfileImage(avatarFile, 'avatar') : Promise.resolve(null),
641	      certFile  ? uploadProfileImage(certFile,  'pesticide-cert') : Promise.resolve(null),
642	    ])
643	    if (error) { setLoading(false); return } // uploadProfileImage sets error on failure
644	
645	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
646	      name:             name.trim(),
647	      village:          village.trim(),
648	      district:         district.trim(),
649	      method,
650	      slug:             newSlug,
651	      pickup_locations: pickupLocations,
652	      cover_photo_url:  (newCoverUrl ?? existingCoverUrl) || null,
653	      photo_url:        (newAvatarUrl ?? existingAvatarUrl) || null,
654	      pesticide_cert_url: (newCertUrl ?? existingCertUrl) || null,
655	      pickup_slots: slotDays.length > 0
656	        ? { days: slotDays, time_from: slotFrom, time_to: slotTo }
657	        : null,
658	      lat: farmerLat,
659	      lng: farmerLng,
660	      location_name: farmerLat ? (farmerLocationName || name.trim()) : null,
661	    }
662	    if (sinceYear) payload.farming_since_year = Number(sinceYear)
663	
664	    const { data, error: err } = await supabase
665	      .from('farmers')
666	      .update(payload)
667	      .eq('id', farmer.id)
668	      .select('*')
669	      .single()
670	
671	    setLoading(false)
672	
673	    if (err || !data) {
674	      setError(err?.message ?? tx.couldNotSave)
675	      return
676	    }
677	
678	    localStorage.setItem('yff_farmer_slug', data.slug)
679	    onSaved(data)
680	  }
681	
682	  return (
683	    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
684	      <div className="bg-white rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
685	        <div className="sticky top-0 bg-white flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-100">
686	          <div>
687	            <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base">
688	              {tx.profileModalTitle}
689	            </h3>
690	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{tx.profileModalSubtitle}</p>
691	          </div>
692	          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-3xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
693	        </div>
694	
695	        <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
696	          <Field
697	            label={tx.yourNameLabel}
698	            placeholder="Ramu Reddy"
699	            value={name}

1901	    const hrs = Math.floor(mins / 60)
1902	    if (hrs < 24) return `${hrs}h ago`
1903	    return `${Math.floor(hrs / 24)}d ago`
1904	  }

1908

1916	            </p>

1918	              <a href={`tel:+91${order.buyer_phone}`} className="text-xs font-semibold text-green-700">

1920	              </a>
1921	            )}
1922	          </div>

1928	                {isPaid ? `✓ ${tx.paymentReceived}` : tx.codBadge}
1929	              </span>
1930	            )}

1932	              {timeAgo(order.created_at)}
1933	            </span>

1935	        </div>
1936	
1937	        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-sm">
1938	          <span className="font-semibold text-gray-800">{order.produce_name || '—'}</span>

1986	export function FreshnessBadge({ harvestDate }: { harvestDate: string }) {
1987	  const { tx } = useLang()
1988	  const days = Math.floor(
1989	    (Date.now() - new Date(harvestDate).getTime()) / 86400000,
1990	  )
1991	  if (days < 0) return null
1992	  if (days === 0) return (
1993	    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-green-700 bg-green-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
1994	      {tx.harvestToday}
1995	    </span>
1996	  )
1997	  if (days === 1) return (
1998	    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
1999	      {tx.harvestYesterday}
2000	    </span>
2001	  )
2002	  return (
2003	    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-red-600 bg-red-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
2004	      {tx.harvestDaysAgo.replace('{n}', String(days))}
2005	    </span>
2006	  )
2007	}

2063	        </>
2064	      )}

}

WhatsApp click analytics (insert-only, no auth needed)\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wa_clicks (\n  id         uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,\n  farmer_id  uuid        REFERENCES farmers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\n  created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()\n);\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_wa_clicks_farmer ON wa_clicks(farmer_id);\n\n-- Allow anonymous inserts for wa_clicks (consumer clicks)\nALTER TABLE wa_clicks ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nCREATE POLICY IF NOT EXISTS \"anon insert wa_clicks\"\n  ON wa_clicks FOR INSERT TO anon WITH CHECK (true);"

> AGENT

Clean build. Here's the full review report:

---

## Review Complete — 7 Bugs Fixed

### Critical bugs fixed

| # | Bug | Impact | Fix |
|---|-----|--------|-----|
| 1 | **FreshnessBadge timezone bug** | Before 5:30 AM IST, "harvested today" badge vanished entirely. `new Date("2024-01-15")` parses as UTC midnight, not local midnight | Moved to `/components/FreshnessBadge.tsx`, now uses `new Date(date + 'T00:00:00')` for local parsing |
| 2 | **Items count wrong after distance filter** | Header said "5 items" but only 2 visible — using `filtered.length` instead of `displayItems.length` | Fixed to `displayItems.length` |
| 3 | **Photo upload error check race condition** | If cover/avatar/cert upload failed, `if (error)` checked React state that hadn't updated yet — profile saved with broken URLs | `uploadProfileImage` now returns `{url, err}` instead of calling `setError()` |
| 4 | **`FreshnessBadge` imported from a page file** | `consumer/page.tsx` and `ProduceTab.tsx` both imported from `@/app/farmer/dashboard/page` — loads entire dashboard module into consumer bundle | Moved to shared `@/components/FreshnessBadge.tsx` |
| 5 | **`wa_clicks` table missing from migration** | Every WhatsApp order click silently errored in the DB | Added table + RLS policy to migration SQL |
| 6 | **Search had no AbortController** | Rapid typing caused stale results to overwrite fresh ones | Added `AbortController`, cancels previous request on new keystroke |
| 7 | **No viewport meta / iOS CartFAB hidden under home bar** | FAB at `bottom-5` sits under iOS notch; no `maximum-scale=1` meant pinch-zoom broke layout | Added `Viewport` export to `layout.tsx`; FAB now uses `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` |

---

### Run this SQL in Supabase tonight (before going live)

```sql
-- scripts/feature-migration.sql  (run the full file)
```
The migration now includes all 11 steps including `password_hash`, `media` table, and `wa_clicks`.

---

### Tomorrow — test these 5 flows on a real Android phone

1. **Farmer login** — new number (creates account) → dashboard loads → edit profile → set GPS location → save
2. **Consumer flow** — set location via GPS → filter `< 10 km` → add to cart from 2 different farmers → send WhatsApp order → check `/consumer/orders` with same phone
3. **Harvest badge** — add a listing with today's harvest date → confirm green "Picked today" badge shows on consumer page
4. **Photo upload** — upload cover photo and avatar in profile edit → confirm it shows on public `/farmer/[slug]` page
5. **Order approval** — consumer sends order → farmer approves on dashboard → farmer marks COD paid → check earnings card updates

> AGENT

`CREATE POLICY` doesn't support `IF NOT EXISTS` — only `CREATE TABLE/INDEX` do. The workaround wraps it in a `DO` block that checks `pg_policies` first. Run the full SQL file again now.
